MA	<p>2315_6a01</p> <p>Restriction enzymes are proteins that cut DNA at specific sequences. <span style="color: #00775f;"><i>PagI</i></span> is derived from Pseudomonas alcaligenes Sau 14-027, and <span style="color: #0067cc;"><i>BfaI</i></span> is derived from Bacteroides fragilis. Both enzymes are labeled on the map.</p><p><strong>DNA Strand Question:</strong></p><p>Examine the DNA strand shown below.
